Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any spin module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 480. The projective resolution has length at most 407 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_480(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 480. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^407(S, F) is trivial. QED.
